From the dialogue so far and your candidates from the previous turn, list the disorders this patient could have.\n\n[Current Turn]\n13\n\n[Previous Candidates]\nF31.1, F30.1\n\n[Dialogue History]\nDoctor: Hello, and welcome. I'm Dr. [Name], a psychiatrist. What brings you in to see me today?\n\nPatient: Hi. Well, I guess what brought me in is that things got pretty out of hand with work recently. I do landscaping, and I went from just being a normal crew guy to... well, suddenly trying to run three different bids all at once. I was calling city people, lining up plant suppliers, and messaging just about everybody I knew about this big park idea I had. It was just like I couldn’t shut it off.\n\nDoctor: How are you feeling mood-wise right now, today? Are you still feeling that same rush of energy and excitement, or has that changed?\n\nPatient: No, it's definitely changed. I don't have that rush anymore at all. Um, today I just feel... I don't know, mostly just really drained. That whole energetic feeling is completely gone now.\n\nDoctor: When you say you feel drained today, have you been feeling this way continuously since that energetic period ended, or did you have some normal days in between?\n\nPatient: Um, I'm not really sure, to be honest. It's kind of hard to tell. I don't really remember having any normal days in between... it mostly just feels like once all that energy wore off, I just went straight into feeling like this. But I wasn't exactly keeping track of the days, so I can't say for sure.\n\nDoctor: When that energetic feeling started to fade, did your mood drop down into feeling sad, hopeless, or depressed, or did you just lose the energy while your mood stayed relatively neutral?\n\nPatient: I'm not really sure how to answer that, honestly. Um... I don't know if I'd call it sad or hopeless, exactly. It's more just... well, like I said, I just felt completely drained. I didn't really notice if my mood was neutral or depressed. I just know I didn't have anything left in the tank.\n\nDoctor: During this drained period you're in now, have you been having thoughts that things are hopeless, or that you're worthless or to blame for things?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I have. Um... I just keep telling myself I'm a worthless idiot, honestly. I put the business and my name on the line when I was all fired up before... and now I just feel like I deserve to lose everything, just because I got way too full of myself.\n\nDoctor: When you were in that energetic, fired-up period with all the business plans, did you feel depressed or down at all during that time, or was that period purely energetic and high-feeling?\n\nPatient: Um, no, I don't think I felt down at all then. It was pretty much all just... high energy, feeling like I could take on the world. I didn't feel depressed during that time, just really fired up and ready to go.\n\nDoctor: During that time when you were fired up with all the energy and business plans, did you experience any moments of feeling irritable, angry, or frustrated—even though you were generally feeling high and energized?\n\nPatient: Yeah, actually, I did. Um... like I said, I was feeling really fired up, almost like I was bigger than myself, you know? But... if one of the guys I work with questioned my plans or told me I needed to slow down, I'd just snap at them. And I'd snap way harder than made sense for what they actually said. It was just... really frustrating when anyone got in my way.\n\nDoctor: When you think back to when that fired-up energy started to fade away, did you notice any days or even a week or two where you felt pretty much back to normal—like your usual self—before you started feeling this drained way you feel now?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. It felt like I just crashed. One day I was going a hundred miles an hour, and then... it just kind of fell apart and I was right down at the bottom. I don't remember any time in between where I felt normal.\n\nDoctor: During this drained period you're in now, have you experienced any beliefs that felt absolutely true to you but that other people might think are unusual or strange—like feeling like you have special powers, or that people are out to get you, or anything like that?\n\nPatient: No, nothing like that. I just feel incredibly tired and empty. I don't think I have any special powers or that anyone's out to get me. I'm just... really dragging, you know? Just completely drained.\n\nDoctor: I want to make sure I have the timeline right. From the time you were feeling that high energy and fired up, to right now feeling completely drained—how many days or weeks has it been total?\n\nPatient: I honestly couldn't give you an exact number. It's just hard to focus on the calendar when I'm this exhausted, you know? I just really don't know the total.\n\nDoctor: When you were in that high-energy, fired-up period before you crashed, did you sleep less than usual but still feel rested and fine, or did you also feel like you needed less sleep?\n\nPatient: I'm not really sure. Like I said, that whole time is just kind of a blur to me right now.\n\nDoctor: I know you said time has been a blur, but let me ask it a different way: After you crashed from that fired-up energy, before you started feeling this drained and worthless, was there any stretch of time—even just a few days—where you felt like your normal self again, or did the bad feelings start pretty much right away?\n\nPatient: I'm really not sure. Um... I don't remember feeling normal in between. It feels like I just crashed and the bad feelings started pretty much right away, but honestly, I just can't say for sure. My head is just too foggy right now to remember if there were any normal days in there.\n\nDoctor: When you think about deserving to lose everything because you got too full of yourself—do you absolutely believe that's true, or do you recognize that thought might be the depression talking and not actually how things really are?\n\nPatient: I don't know... I mean, it feels true. It really feels like it's my fault and I brought it all on myself. But... I guess I don't know for sure if it's just the depression talking. It's just hard to separate it out right now.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out. I put the business and my name on the line when I was all fired up before... and now I just feel like I deserve to lose everything, just because I got way too full of myself.\n\nDoctor: When you were in that energetic, fired-up period with all the business plans, did you feel depressed or down at all during that time, or was that period purely energetic and high-feeling?\n\nPatient: Um, no, I don't think I felt down at all then. It was pretty much all just... high energy, feeling like I could take on the world. I didn't feel depressed during that time, just really fired up and ready to go.\n\nDoctor: During that time when you were fired up with all the energy and business plans, did you experience any moments of feeling irritable, angry, or frustrated—even though you were generally feeling high and energized?\n\nPatient: Yeah, actually, I did. Um... like I said, I was feeling really fired up, almost like I was bigger than myself, you know? But... if one of the guys I work with questioned my plans or told me I needed to slow down, I'd just snap at them. And I'd snap way harder than made sense for what they actually said. It was just... really frustrating when anyone got in my way.\n\nDoctor: When you think back to when that fired-up energy started to fade away, did you notice any days or even a week or two where you felt pretty much back to normal—like your usual self—before you started feeling this drained way you feel now?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. It was just... really frustrating when anyone got in my way.\n\nDoctor: When you think back to when that fired-up energy started to fade away, did you notice any days or even a week or two where you felt pretty much back to normal—like your usual self—before you started feeling this drained way you feel now?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. It felt like I just crashed. One day I was going a hundred miles an hour, and then... it just kind of fell apart and I was right down at the bottom. I don't remember any time in between where I felt normal.\n\nDoctor: During this drained period you're in now, have you experienced any beliefs that felt absolutely true to you but that other people might think are unusual or strange—like feeling like you have special powers, or that people are out to get you, or anything like that?\n\nPatient: No, nothing like that. I just feel incredibly tired and empty. I don't think I have any special powers or that anyone's out to get me. I'm just... really dragging, you know? Just completely drained.\n\nDoctor: I want to make sure I have the timeline right. I just really don't know the total.\n\nDoctor: When you were in that high-energy, fired-up period before you crashed, did you sleep less than usual but still feel rested and fine, or did you also feel like you needed less sleep?\n\nPatient: I'm not really sure. I don't remember any time in between where I felt normal.\n\nDoctor: During this drained period you're in now, have you experienced any beliefs that felt absolutely true to you but that other people might think are unusual or strange—like feeling like you have special powers, or that people are out to get you, or anything like that?\n\nPatient: No, nothing like that. I just feel incredibly tired and empty. I don't think I have any special powers or that anyone's out to get me. I'm just... really dragging, you know? But I wasn't exactly keeping track of the days, so I can't say for sure.\n\nDoctor: When that energetic feeling started to fade, did your mood drop down into feeling sad, hopeless, or depressed, or did you just lose the energy while your mood stayed relatively neutral?\n\nPatient: I'm not really sure how to answer that, honestly. Um... I don't know if I'd call it sad or hopeless, exactly. It's more just... well, like I said, I just felt completely drained. I didn't really notice if my mood was neutral or depressed. I just know I didn't have anything left in the tank.\n\nDoctor: During this drained period you're in now, have you been having thoughts that things are hopeless, or that you're worthless or to blame for things?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I have. Um... I just keep telling myself I'm a worthless idiot, honestly. I put the business and my name on the line when I was all fired up before... and now I just feel like I deserve to lose everything, just because I got way too full of myself.\n\nDoctor: When you were in that energetic, fired-up period with all the business plans, did you feel depressed or down at all during that time, or was that period purely energetic and high-feeling?\n\nPatient: Um, no, I don't think I felt down at all then. It was pretty much all just... high energy, feeling like I could take on the world. I didn't feel depressed during that time, just really fired up and ready to go.\n\nDoctor: During that time when you were fired up with all the energy and business plans, did you experience any moments of feeling irritable, angry, or frustrated—even though you were generally feeling high and energized?\n\nPatient: Yeah, actually, I did. Um... like I said, I was feeling really fired up, almost like I was bigger than myself, you know? But... if one of the guys I work with questioned my plans or told me I needed to slow down, I'd just snap at them. And I'd snap way harder than made sense for what they actually said. It was just... really frustrating when anyone got in my way.\n\nDoctor: When you think back to when that fired-up energy started to fade away, did you notice any days or even a week or two where you felt pretty much back to normal—like your usual self—before you started feeling this drained way you feel now?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. It felt like I just crashed. One day I was going a hundred miles an hour, and then... it just kind of fell apart and I was right down at the bottom. I don't remember any time in between where I felt normal.\n\nDoctor: During this drained period you're in now, have you experienced any beliefs that felt absolutely true to you but that other people might think are unusual or strange—like feeling like you have special powers, or that people are out to get you, or anything like that?\n\nPatient: No, nothing like that. I just feel incredibly tired and empty. I don't think I have any special powers or that anyone's out to get me. I'm just... really dragging, you know? Just completely drained.\n\nDoctor: I want to make sure I have the timeline right. From the time you were feeling that high energy and fired up, to right now feeling completely drained—how many days or weeks has it been total?\n\nPatient: I honestly couldn't give you an exact number. Um... time has just been kind of a blur lately. I know I was going non-stop for a bit, and then I hit that wall, but... I haven't really been keeping track of the days or weeks. It's just hard to focus on the calendar when I'm this exhausted, you know? I just really don't know the total.\n\nDoctor: When you were in that high-energy, fired-up period before you crashed, did you sleep less than usual but still feel rested and fine, or did you also feel like you needed less sleep?\n\nPatient: I'm not really sure. I mean... I don't remember thinking I needed less sleep, or really paying much attention to it. I might have slept less, but I just didn't notice it either way. Like I said, that whole time is just kind of a blur to me right now.\n\nDoctor: I know you said time has been a blur, but let me ask it a different way: After you crashed from that fired-up energy, before you started feeling this drained and worthless, was there any stretch of time—even just a few days—where you felt like your normal self again, or did the bad feelings start pretty much right away?\n\nPatient: I'm really not sure. Um... I don't remember feeling normal in between. It feels like I just crashed and the bad feelings started pretty much right away, but honestly, I just can't say for sure. My head is just too foggy right now to remember if there were any normal days in there.\n\nDoctor: When you think about deserving to lose everything because you got too full of yourself—do you absolutely believe that's true, or do you recognize that thought might be the depression talking and not actually how things really are?\n\nPatient: I don't know... I mean, it feels true. It really feels like it's my fault and I brought it all on myself. But... I guess I don't know for sure if it's just the depression talking. It's just hard to separate it out right now.\n\nDoctor: Right after that high-energy period ended and before you started feeling this drained way—even if it was just for one day—did you have any time where your mood and energy felt completely back to normal, like before all this started?\n\nPatient: I really don't think so. I mean, I can't say for absolute certain, but... no, I don't remember any normal days in between. It just felt like the energy completely dropped out from under me and I went straight into feeling this low. There wasn't any kind of break.\n\nDoctor: I know you've said you don't remember normal days, but let me ask more specifically: in those first few days right after you stopped feeling that fired-up energy, before the really low feelings set in, did your mood and energy level feel like they were back to how you normally feel when you're not going through anything unusual?\n\nPatient: I really don't have a different answer for you on that. I... I know you're asking about those specific few days, but I just don't remember feeling regular at all. When that wired feeling shut off, the crash just happened. There wasn't any middle ground in there where I felt like my usual self.\n\nDoctor: When you were in that high-energy, fired-up period—before it all came crashing down—did you ever feel angry or frustrated at people who questioned you, but your mood and energy were still high and energized during those moments?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I did. Before the crash happened, when I was feeling really fired up and... I don't know, almost bigger than myself, I'd get really annoyed if someone got in my way. Like, if a coworker questioned my plan for a job or told me I needed to slow down, I'd just snap at him. Way harder than made sense for what they were saying. I was still full of all that energy, but I'd just get so frustrated with them for not keeping up or for questioning me.\n\nDoctor: I want to make absolutely sure about the transition. In those first couple of days right after that fired-up, energetic feeling started to wear off—before you hit the really low point—did your mood and energy feel completely normal and baseline, like how you feel on a regular day when nothing unusual is happening?\n\nPatient: I... Doc, I really don't know how else to say it. You've asked me this a few times now, and the answer is still the same. No. I didn't feel normal. There were no regular days in between. The energy just... it just stopped, and I crashed. That's really all I can tell you.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out. Look, I know you're trying to get the timeline straight, but... no. Not a single day. I don't remember any time in between where I felt normal.\n\nDoctor: During this drained period you're in now, have you experienced any beliefs that felt absolutely true to you but that other people might think are unusual or strange—like feeling like you have special powers, or that people are out to get you, or anything like that?\n\nPatient: No, nothing like that. I mean, it feels true. It really feels like it's my fault and I brought it all on myself. But... I guess I don't know for sure if it's just the depression talking. It's just hard to separate it out right now.\n\nDoctor: Right after that high-energy period ended and before you started feeling this drained way—even if it was just for one day—did you have any time where your mood and energy felt completely back to normal, like before all this started?\n\nPatient: I really don't think so. I mean, I can't say for absolute certain, but... no, I don't remember any normal days in between. It just felt like the energy completely dropped out from under me and I went straight into feeling this low. There wasn't any kind of break.\n\nDoctor: I know you've said you don't remember normal days, but let me ask more specifically: in those first few days right after you stopped feeling that fired-up energy, before the really low feelings set in, did your mood and energy level feel like they were back to how you normally feel when you're not going through anything unusual?\n\nPatient: I really don't have a different answer for you on that.
